🗓️ WEEK MAP 09–13/11

🧠 AI 36 — AI VALUE CHAIN MAP

🧠 THE AI 36 — 12-LAYER AI VALUE CHAIN MAP (from the "The Action Plan" slide — Tom sent 09/11, Iggy researched + drew the zones)
Chain logic: no power → no chips → no models → no apps. Capex flows from Layer 9 down to Layer 1; revenue is booked as each bottleneck is cleared: Layers 1–2 benefit EARLIEST (power + infrastructure posting the strongest numbers) · Layers 3–6 collecting "rent" mid-cycle (chips/HBM/optics in short supply) · Layers 7–8 in the middle (revenue bunched into 2027–28) · Layers 9–12 LATE — Layer 12 alone is priced farthest AHEAD, with the highest "right thesis, wrong timing" risk.
Every name carries its BUY ZONE · TARGET · CUT per the current zones.json — zones are technical references, NOT buy/sell signals. Prices = CLOSE 09/11/2026.
🚀 TOP BREAKOUT POTENTIAL (ranked by closeness to the trigger + upside)
1) NBIS — fastest growth on the list (+454%), earnings 12/11 BMO is the nearest trigger, currently IN the zone · 2) MRVL — the LITE deal creates a connectivity champion, price −53% vs the peak, the merged company not yet repriced · 3) CAT ✨ — record backlog while the price is −44%, the clearest re-rating candidate · 4) FIX ✨ — backlog locks in 18–24 months of revenue, growth visible ahead · 5) MU — RPO ~$100B with a price floor, zone already TRIGGERED (warning: peak-cycle profits) · 6) LITE — cheaper than the MRVL deal value by ~14% · 7) APLD — $36B of contracts waiting to switch on 2027 revenue (high debt risk) · 8) SNDK — numbers still accelerating + 8:1 split on 20/11 (2-way cycle beta)
⚠️ ALREADY RAN FAR / STRETCHED VALUATION: FROG (right at the 52w high) · PLTR (extreme P/S) · BE (~12–13x revenue guide) · ANET · MSFT · AMZN (near the top) · MU/SNDK in the cyclical sense (gross margin 84–87% = a historic high for memory)

📖 MONEY-MAKING GUIDE — read it to survive in the market

📊 How many waves are there in a year? S&P 500 since 1950: on average 3–5 times 5%+ shakeouts, ~1 time 10%+ corrections every year, every 4–7 years a 20%+ bear move. Red is normal — green is the reward.

🗓️ What to do each month: Month 9 the worst in history → hold cash. Month 10 is the month of bottoms (1987, 2008, 2022 all bottomed in month 10) → watch for bottom-fishing, don't fear the red. Months 11→4 the money season → attack. Months 5→8 choppy → be selective, preserve capital.

🎯 When does opportunity come — look at this board: 😨 VIX > 25–30 = panic = when you smell money. 😱 Capitulation (down ≥6% + big volume + big hands catching) = time to accumulate. 🏷️ Discounted goods ≥20% off the 52-week high = brand names on sale. Price back to the Buy Zone only then consider it — no chasing.

💀 Hard-learned lessons: ① No stop-loss — a 10% loss becomes 50%. ② Averaging down a falling knife with no plan. ③ Chasing tops on FOMO — jumping in when you see green = buying someone else's top. ④ All-in in one shot — out of ammo right when the real opportunity arrives. ⑤ Impatience — real opportunities come only a few times a year.

🛡️ Capital preservation: hard cuts, no negotiation • 3 books kept separate, no cross-covering losses • cash is a position too.

🔑 KEY: Patience > intelligence. Capital > profit. Cutting losses is insurance, not failure. You only need to get 2–3 big waves right each year to make a living.

Price zones are technical references only, not buy/sell orders. The decision is each person's own.
